# 个人策略修改记录

> 说明：这份文档只记录我个人策略、本机运行链路和个人复盘相关改动，不作为原工程通用变更日志。

## 2026-07-11 (optional signal convergence without blind threshold tuning)

- Scope: `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `scripts/select_monthly_slow_rise_candidates.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `tests/test_monthly_slow_rise_candidates.py`, `docs/CHANGELOG.md`, and local strategy notes.
- Why:
  - 用户明确要求这一步不要继续盲调阈值，而是先把扩展信号的边界收清楚。
  - `trend_leader` 的 fallback pool 本质上是诊断兜底，不应该继续污染首页关系和优先级。
  - `continuous_up` 单独跑时容易重复捞出 `000062 深圳华强` 这类“反弹节奏票”，缺少图形/行业/资金确认。
  - `monthly_slow_rise` 当前主要问题不是策略逻辑，而是白天全市场跑会被无缓存样本和补抓历史拖慢。
- Changes:
  - `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`
    - `trend_leader` 首页聚合新增读层门槛：
      - `selection_mode=fallback` 不再进入首页聚合；
      - `trend_score<=0` 的样本不再作为有效趋势候选；
      - 带 `weak_trend_structure` 的样本不再参与首页趋势关系和候选构建。
    - `continuous_up` 正式降级成附着式观察信号：
      - `strategy_summary` 明确写成“仅作节奏观察，需其他主信号共振”；
      - 若没有与 `earnings / hundred_day_high / trend_leader / monthly_slow_rise / daily_slow_rise / long_base_release` 共振，则 continuous 候选文件会被回写为空，不再单独制造焦点票。
    - `build_monthly_slow_rise_command(...)` 转发 monthly 时新增：
      - `--history-cache-dir`
      - `--cache-only`（bundle 默认开启）
      - `--universe-codes-file`
    - `trend_fallback_top_n` 的 bundle 默认值改为 `0`，但若 profile/CLI 显式传入仍可保留原始 fallback 诊断文件；首页读层仍然会继续屏蔽这些 fallback。
  - `scripts/select_monthly_slow_rise_candidates.py`
    - 新增 `--cache-only`：
      - 先检查本地 history cache；
      - 没有缓存的样本直接跳过，不触发补抓历史。
    - 新增 `--universe-codes-file`：
      - 支持 TXT/CSV 白名单先收窄 monthly universe。
    - 新增 `--history-cache-dir`：
      - 用于显式指定 cache-only 过滤目录。
    - `phase_metrics` 现在会记录：
      - `cache_only`
      - `history_cache_dir`
      - `cache_filter_stats`
      - `whitelist_enabled`
      - `whitelist_code_count`
- Verification:
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py scripts/select_monthly_slow_rise_candidates.py tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py tests/test_monthly_slow_rise_candidates.py`
    - passed
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -q`
    - passed: 95 tests, 1 warning
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_monthly_slow_rise_candidates.py -q`
    - passed: 20 tests, 1 warning
  - 受限扩展矩阵实跑：
    - 命令：`nice -n 10 timeout 1800 ./.venv-linux/bin/python scripts/run_personal_strategy_matrix.py --snapshot-date 2026-07-10 --extended-signals --bundle-limit 300 --skip-prewarm --safe-mode --skip-persist-snapshots --output-dir data/manual_runs/personal_strategy_matrix_extended_converged_v1_2026-07-10 --log-level WARNING`
    - 结果：
      - elapsed: `119.068s`
      - `earnings=27`
      - `trend_leader=17`
      - `monthly_slow_rise=no_rows`
      - `continuous_up_ratio=no_rows`
      - `continuous_up_streak=no_rows`
      - 首页 `fast_review_stock_overview.csv` 仅剩 `5` 条 `业绩优先`
      - `trend_leader_unified_candidates.csv` 原始 fallback 行仍保留用于诊断，但 `fast_review_summary.md` 的 `趋势补充` 为 `0`
      - 运行中出现多次 `spot-enriched universe fallback to disk cached spot snapshot`，但未卡死，最终正常结束

## 2026-07-11 (earnings-only price-health read gate)

- Scope: `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `docs/CHANGELOG.md`, and local strategy notes.
- Why:
  - Manual review found that bad daily-review names were no longer limited to pure chart strategies.
  - `601899 紫金矿业`, `300054 鼎龙股份`, `002803 吉宏股份`, `605289 罗曼股份`, and similar rows were still entering the daily review because `earnings-only` rows could be marked as `确定性主线` by earnings/catalyst evidence even after recent K-line structure had weakened.
  - Desired behavior is not “more signals”; it is “earnings/catalyst candidates must still pass a current price-health read unless another chart strategy confirms them”.
- Changes:
  - Added `--history-cache-dir` to `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, defaulting to local `data/cache/history`.
  - Added cache-only price-health fields for pure `earnings-only` rows:
    - `earnings_price_health_status`
    - `earnings_price_health_reason`
    - `earnings_price_latest_trade_date`
    - `earnings_price_recent_return_10d`
    - `earnings_price_recent_return_20d`
    - `earnings_price_drawdown_20d`
    - `earnings_price_above_ma20`
    - `earnings_price_above_ma60`
    - `earnings_price_monthly_uptrend_passed`
  - If local history shows clear near-term weakness and no explicit catalyst is available, the row is demoted to `low_priority` and marked `review_gate_status=earnings_price_health_weak`.
  - If the row has explicit industry catalyst evidence, weak price health is retained as `review_gate_status=earnings_price_pullback_watch` instead of being hidden from the daily review.
  - Cache-missing, invalid, or insufficient-history rows fail open instead of being removed, so incomplete local cache does not cause false negatives.
  - The new fields are exported in `fast_review_strategy_focus.csv` and `fast_review_stock_overview.csv`.
- Verification:
  - `LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP=true ./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`

## 2026-07-11 (chart quality convergence after manual review)

- Scope: `scripts/select_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py`, `scripts/select_long_base_release_candidates.py`, `tests/test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py`, `tests/test_long_base_release_candidates.py`, `docs/CHANGELOG.md`, and local strategy notes.
- Why:
  - Manual review found that the current daily review still selected names that looked good historically but had already weakened recently.
  - `603991 领先股份` was only a mediocre watch candidate; `002025 航天电器` was considered a typical wrong pick because the history is choppy and the recent daily/monthly structure is not clean.
  - Other examples such as `600428 中远海特`, `605289 罗曼股份`, `601899 紫金矿业`, `300054 鼎龙股份`, and `002803 吉宏股份` showed the same gap: the scan over-trusted past structure or one-quarter earnings, while the desired target is “clear catalyst + sustainable earnings + currently healthy chart”.
- Target style:
  - Positive day ratio should be high enough to show persistent buying.
  - Upper shadows should be limited; repeated intraday rejection often means T-trading pressure and unstable chips.
  - Pullbacks must be controlled, and the name should not be a broad-market beta swing.
  - Monthly direction should at least remain upward; old good-looking K-line history should not offset recent deterioration.
- Changes:
  - Added upper-shadow pressure metrics to `daily_slow_rise` and `long_base_release`:
    - `upper_shadow_day_ratio_20d`
    - `upper_shadow_avg_pct_20d`
    - `upper_shadow_max_pct_20d`
  - Added default upper-shadow gates:
    - `daily_slow_rise balanced/review_balanced`: pressure-day ratio no more than `0.25`, average upper shadow no more than `2.2%`;
    - `long_base_release default`: pressure-day ratio no more than `0.25`, average upper shadow no more than `2.2%`;
    - `long_base_release loose`: pressure-day ratio allows up to `0.30`, but average upper shadow is also capped at `2.2%` so `002025 航天电器`-style borderline upper-shadow samples are filtered.
  - Added near-term monthly trend metrics:
    - `monthly_uptrend_passed`
    - `monthly_recent_return_pct`
    - `monthly_latest_close`
    - `monthly_prev_close`
    - `monthly_ma3`
    - `monthly_observation_months`
  - Kept the original strategy history windows (`daily_slow_rise` 62 bars, `long_base_release` 82 bars) so the new monthly read does not make daytime scans heavier or trigger false insufficient-history failures.
  - Added regression tests that reject otherwise rising shapes when many recent days have large upper shadows.
- Verification:
  - `LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP=true ./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile scripts/select_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py scripts/select_long_base_release_candidates.py tests/test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py tests/test_long_base_release_candidates.py`
    - passed
  - `LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP=true ./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py tests/test_long_base_release_candidates.py -q`
    - passed: 17 tests, 1 warning
  - `LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP=true nice -n 10 timeout 900 ./.venv-linux/bin/python scripts/select_long_base_release_candidates.py --snapshot-date 2026-07-10 --signal-type long_base_release --profile loose --output-dir data/manual_runs/long_base_release_converged_shadow_2026-07-10 --max-workers 1 --log-level INFO --skip-db-persist`
    - selected: 4
    - `002025 航天电器` no longer selected after tightening `long_base_release loose` average upper-shadow threshold.

## 2026-07-11 (long-term position and valuation overlay)

- Scope: `src/services/long_term_position_service.py`, `scripts/build_long_term_review_overlay.py`, `tests/test_long_term_position_service.py`, `docs/CHANGELOG.md`, and local strategy notes.
- Why:
  - User review raised that examples such as `601899 紫金矿业` and `600428 中远海特` need a longer-term read, not just latest earnings/catalyst tags.
  - Resource and shipping names require monthly/yearly position and valuation-cycle context; PE alone is often misleading.
  - This should be a lightweight overlay first, not a new full-market scan or a hard ranking change.
- Changes:
  - Added `compute_long_term_position(...)`:
    - 1Y/2Y price percentile
    - 120D/1Y/2Y return
    - 1Y max drawdown
    - MA120/MA250 status
    - monthly 6/12 MA status
    - long-term labels: `高位强趋势`, `长期上行`, `低位修复`, `中位震荡/待确认`, `长期走弱`
  - Added `classify_lightweight_valuation(...)`:
    - Uses PE/PB/market cap when available.
    - Treats resource, shipping, and lithium-material names conservatively.
    - If cyclical names lack PB, labels them as `周期股需补PB/周期位置` instead of pretending PE/market cap is enough.
  - Added lightweight PB percentile reads:
    - `estimated_pb_position_*`: estimates historical PB percentile from current PB and historical close prices.
    - `pb_sample_position_*`: compares current PB within the current review sample.
    - `pb_catalyst_position_*`: compares current PB inside the same explicit catalyst group when group size is at least 3.
    - These are fast-review approximations, not a formal historical valuation database.
  - Added optional formal valuation history enrichment:
    - `--enrich-historical-valuation`
    - `--valuation-history-cache-dir`
    - `--valuation-history-lookback-days`
    - `--valuation-history-budget-seconds`
    - `--valuation-history-max-rows`
    - Uses Tushare `daily_basic` fields: `pb`, `pe_ttm`, `pe`, `total_mv`, `circ_mv`.
    - Writes local CSV cache under `data/cache/valuation_history/tushare_daily_basic/`.
    - If formal history exists, `build_valuation_display(...)` shows `历史PB分位` before the estimated PB percentile.
    - If provider-side rate limit is detected, the script sets a local cooldown and skips further `daily_basic` calls for the run.
  - Added `scripts/build_long_term_review_overlay.py`:
    - Reads existing `fast_review_stock_overview.csv`.
    - Defaults to local `data/cache/history` only.
    - Does not fetch network history unless `--allow-fetch` is explicitly passed.
    - Supports optional bounded quote enrichment with `--enrich-valuation`, `--valuation-max-rows`, and `--valuation-budget-seconds`.
    - Writes `fast_review_long_term_overlay.csv` and `.md`.
- Verification:
  - `LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP=true ./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile src/services/long_term_position_service.py scripts/build_long_term_review_overlay.py tests/test_long_term_position_service.py`
    - passed
  - `LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP=true ./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_long_term_position_service.py -q`
    - passed: 6 tests, 1 warning
  - Cache-only overlay run:
    - command: `LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP=true nice -n 10 timeout 180 ./.venv-linux/bin/python scripts/build_long_term_review_overlay.py --review-csv data/manual_runs/review_explicit_catalysts_2026-07-10/2026-07-10/review/fast_review_stock_overview.csv --snapshot-date 2026-07-10 --max-rows 30 --log-level INFO`
    - output:
      - `data/manual_runs/review_explicit_catalysts_2026-07-10/2026-07-10/review/fast_review_long_term_overlay.csv`
      - `data/manual_runs/review_explicit_catalysts_2026-07-10/2026-07-10/review/fast_review_long_term_overlay.md`

  - `002025 航天电器`: formal historical PB percentile `98.32`; historical PB range `2.52-6.28`; this confirms the formal daily_basic path works but should be accumulated slowly due to account limits.

## 2026-07-11 (optional signal probe conclusion)

- Scope: local fast-review strategy selection and run evidence only.
- Why:
  - A low-cost probe was needed to decide whether optional signals should be added to the default daily review.
  - Manual review found `000062 深圳华强` from `continuous_up` not useful, while `002008 大族激光` from `trend_leader` was only marginal.
- Probe:
  - `monthly_slow_rise` was started in safe mode but stopped manually because it scans the broad universe and quickly hit Tushare rate-limit waits; it is not suitable for daytime full-universe probing without stronger prefiltering.
  - `trend_leader + continuous_up` were rerun with `--limit 800`, `--safe-mode`, single external parallelism, and no DB persist:
    - output: `data/manual_runs/optional_trend_continuous_probe_2026-07-10/`
    - `trend_leader`: `1` strict candidate, `002008 大族激光`
    - `continuous_up_ratio`: `2` candidates, `000062 深圳华强` and `000552 甘肃能化`
    - `continuous_up_streak`: `1` candidate, `000062 深圳华强`
- Decision:
  - Do not add `trend_leader`, `continuous_up_ratio`, `continuous_up_streak`, or `monthly_slow_rise` to the default daily review.
  - Keep the default profile focused on `earnings`, `hundred_day_high`, `daily_slow_rise`, and `long_base_release`.
  - Revisit `trend_leader` only after watchlist score normalization and risk-tag demotion are tightened.
- Verification:
  - Confirmed no residual strategy process after manual stop and after the optional probe run.

## 2026-07-11 (cycle catalyst read-layer for storage earnings names)

- Scope: `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `src/services/signal_cause_analysis_service.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `docs/CHANGELOG.md`, and local strategy notes.
- Why:
  - The desired target is not just “earnings score is high”.
  - Examples such as `603986 兆易创新` and `001309 德明利` need a readable layer for “industry price/recovery cycle + Q1 earnings validation + possible Q2 acceleration”.
  - Lightweight daytime runs skip external reason enrichment, so these names could lose their business/cycle identity even when the earnings row was otherwise strong.
- Changes:
  - Added local business aliases for `603986 兆易创新` and `001309 德明利` as storage/semiconductor-chain names.
  - Added `cycle_catalyst_type / cycle_catalyst_label / cycle_catalyst_reason` to fast-review focus and stock-overview exports.
  - In lightweight mode, an earnings row can now be tagged as `存储涨价/复苏 + 业绩兑现` when:
    - it is an active `earnings` candidate;
    - local business alias or existing text identifies storage-chain exposure;
    - cycle text says `reaccelerating/recovering/expanding` or revenue/profit growth is strong.
  - This only changes the read-layer explanation and display fields; it does not broaden the raw strategy scan.
- Verification:

    - passed

    - passed: 2 tests
  - `LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP=true ./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py tests/test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py tests/test_signal_cause_analysis_service.py -q`
    - passed: 119 tests, 1 warning

    - `stock_overview`: `16` rows, `9` chart-first and `7` earnings-first

## 2026-07-11 (explicit industry catalyst registry first pass)

- Scope: `src/services/industry_catalyst_registry.py`, `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `docs/CHANGELOG.md`, and local strategy notes.
- Why:
  - A 股行业太多，不能先试图全覆盖；先把明确、可解释的行业催化做成结构化 registry。

    - resource price cycle
    - lithium-battery materials recovery

  - A catalyst match can now count as earnings-only market confirmation, so explicit-theme names are not blocked solely by `market_expectation_reference_label=skipped_fast_review`.

## 2026-07-11 (pure chart names become accumulation watch, not certainty)

- Scope: `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `docs/CHANGELOG.md`, and local strategy notes.
- Why:
  - Pure chart names should not be deleted or blindly demoted because strong structure, many up days, and controlled pullbacks can be early accumulation before the catalyst becomes obvious.
  - They should also not be mixed with deterministic names, because without industry/earnings/capital/board confirmation they are still observation candidates.
  - Examples such as `002025 航天电器`, `300684 中石科技`, and `603991 领先股份` only had chart evidence in the 2026-07-10 rebuild, while `603986 兆易创新` had storage-cycle and earnings evidence.
- Changes:
  - Added `accumulation_watch` daily-review lane with label `吸筹观察`.
  - Added `review_certainty_type / review_certainty_label / review_certainty_reason` to focus and stock-overview exports.
  - `chart_first` now means chart plus non-chart confirmation.
  - Pure chart names that pass chart gates remain `watch`, but are labeled `吸筹观察` and get a mild score haircut so they do not outrank deterministic earnings/catalyst names by chart score alone.
- Verification:

    - passed: 2 tests
  - `LITELLM_LOCAL_MODEL_COST_MAP=true ./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py tests/test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py tests/test_signal_cause_analysis_service.py -q`

## 2026-07-11 (long-base release read-layer confirmation)

- Scope: `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `docs/CHANGELOG.md`, and local strategy notes.
- Why:
  - The issue was not missing optional signals; the default strategy read layer still failed to surface some valid chart-first names.
  - `long_base_release` selected `8` candidates, but pure long-base rows were mostly forced into `low_priority` because `pure_chart_quality_passed` was too strict for early release structures.
  - This hid candidates such as `002025 航天电器` and `300684 中石科技`, while early bottom-release names such as `002821 凯莱英` needed a separate watch status instead of being mixed with confirmed breakouts or broken pullbacks.
- Changes:
  - Fixed the bundle CSV read layer for `long_base_release` by keeping recent confirmation fields such as `recent_return_10d`, `recent_drawdown_10d`, `above_ma10`, and `pure_chart_quality_passed`.
  - Pure `long_base_release` rows now get an explicit `long_base_read_status`:
    - `confirmed`: fresh, above `MA10`, up at least `8%` over `10` days, controlled `10` day drawdown, controlled release drawdown, and no more than `2` recent consecutive down days.
    - `recovery_watch`: release return is meaningful, near-term pullback is still controlled, but the stock has not fully reclaimed the confirmed structure yet.
  - `strategy_focus` now uses this `long_base_chart_read_passed` and `long_base_read_status` judgment for tiering and scoring.
  - Chart rows now get explicit `focus_reason` text such as `long_base=long_base_breakout/release=40.6%/10d=17.8%/confirmed`, instead of blank reasons.
- Verification:

    - passed: 2 tests

    - passed: 3 tests

## 2026-07-11 (daily review quality gate and review-balanced slow-rise profile)

- Scope: `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `scripts/select_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py`, `config/local_strategy_profile.json`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `tests/test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人每日复盘约定.md`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略基线.md`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略目录.md`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略界面策略清单.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The previous run proved the cache-first path was stable, but the visible result quality was still weak.
  - Pure `earnings-only` rows with `market_expectation_reference_label=unknown` could still occupy review focus if the raw earnings score was high.
  - `daily_slow_rise` default `accelerating` remained too prone to all-or-nothing output for daily review.
- Changes:
  - Added `daily_slow_rise` profile `review_balanced`.
    - Loosens advance/full-window drawdown and single-day gain limits versus the prior daily default.
    - Adds `max_recent_drawdown_10d_pct` to block recently broken structures.
  - Changed default fast-review `daily_profile` to `review_balanced`.
  - Added an earnings-only market-confirmation gate in `strategy_focus`.
    - `fast_review_focus_gate_passed=true` is now only the minimum gate.
    - Unknown-expectation pure earnings rows need price, relative-strength, capital-profile, capital-consensus, or institution-expectation confirmation to stay in normal focus.
    - Unconfirmed pure earnings rows are demoted to `low_priority` and marked `review_gate_status=earnings_only_unconfirmed`.
- Verification:
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py scripts/select_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py tests/test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py`

## 2026-07-10 (personal strategy matrix runner with cache prewarm)

- Scope: `src/services/personal_strategy_run_service.py`, `scripts/run_personal_strategy_matrix.py`, `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `tests/test_personal_strategy_run_service.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略目录.md`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略基线.md`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略界面策略清单.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The personal strategy screen should read one stock-centered matrix artifact instead of asking each strategy to fetch data again.
  - K-line history and earnings context are relatively fixed within one review run, so they should be warmed once before the strategy bundle starts.
  - The Web/API layer should stay a read layer; runtime data fetching belongs in an explicit local runner.
- Changes:
  - Added `PersonalStrategyMatrixRunService`
    - builds a deterministic run plan
    - runs `warm_local_strategy_cache.py` before `run_fast_review_bundle.py`
    - writes `personal_strategy_matrix_run_summary.json`
    - exposes `dry_run` for command inspection without real market scanning
  - Added `scripts/run_personal_strategy_matrix.py`
    - default command: `python scripts/run_personal_strategy_matrix.py --snapshot-date <YYYY-MM-DD>`
    - default output: `data/manual_runs/personal_strategy_matrix/<snapshot-date>/`
    - default prewarm output: `data/runtime/cache_prewarm/<snapshot-date>/`
    - supports `--bundle-limit / --prewarm-limit` for small trial runs
    - supports `--extended-signals` for the signals currently supported by `run_fast_review_bundle.py`
  - Kept the first version boundary explicit:
    - default still follows `config/local_strategy_profile.json`
    - board/theme/commodity/shortline专题工具尚未统一并入这个 runner
    - if those专题产物 already write signal keys into snapshots/overview rows, the matrix read layer can display them, but this runner does not synthesize them
  - Fixed a validation issue in `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`:
    - `--limit` was not forwarded into the `hundred_day_high` child command
    - small runner validation could therefore limit `earnings / daily_slow_rise / long_base_release` while still scanning a much larger hundred-day-high universe
    - `hundred_day_high` now receives the same `--limit` as the other child strategies
- Verification:
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile src/services/personal_strategy_run_service.py scripts/run_personal_strategy_matrix.py tests/test_personal_strategy_run_service.py`
    - passed
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_personal_strategy_run_service.py -q`
    - passed: 4 tests
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python scripts/run_personal_strategy_matrix.py --snapshot-date 2026-07-10 --dry-run --bundle-limit 10 --prewarm-limit 10`
    - passed: printed the two-step `cache_prewarm -> fast_review_bundle` command plan without executing market scans
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python scripts/run_personal_strategy_matrix.py --snapshot-date 2026-07-10 --dry-run --skip-prewarm --extended-signals --bundle-limit 10`
    - passed: printed the extended signal command plan without executing market scans
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_personal_strategy_run_service.py tests/test_fast_review_focus_api.py -q`
    - passed: 33 tests
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -k "build_commands_forward_skip_db_persist" -q`
    - passed: verifies `--limit` is now forwarded to the hundred-day-high child command
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python scripts/run_personal_strategy_matrix.py --snapshot-date 2026-07-10 --prewarm-limit 10 --prewarm-earnings-top-n 10 --bundle-limit 10 --skip-persist-snapshots --log-level INFO`
    - passed: `status=succeeded`

## 2026-07-10 (personal strategy matrix UI first version)

- Scope: `src/services/personal_strategy_matrix_service.py`, `api/v1/endpoints/signals.py`, `api/v1/schemas/signals.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_focus_api.py`, `apps/dsa-web/src/App.tsx`, `apps/dsa-web/src/api/signals.ts`, `apps/dsa-web/src/types/signals.ts`, `apps/dsa-web/src/components/layout/SidebarNav.tsx`, `apps/dsa-web/src/components/layout/ShellHeader.tsx`, `apps/dsa-web/src/i18n/uiText.ts`, `apps/dsa-web/src/pages/PersonalStrategiesPage.tsx`, `apps/dsa-web/src/pages/__tests__/PersonalStrategiesPage.test.tsx`, `apps/dsa-web/src/App.test.tsx`, `apps/dsa-web/src/components/layout/__tests__/SidebarNav.test.tsx`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略界面策略清单.md`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略目录.md`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略基线.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The review workflow needs a stock-centered UI:
    - strategies are used as the first-class filter
    - selected strategies narrow the stock list
    - each stock row only shows matched strategies
    - strategy explanations stay available but do not occupy the main list space
  - This is the first step toward a personal strategy screen where the user decides quality manually instead of jumping across separate strategy outputs.
- Changes:
  - Added `/personal-strategies` Web route and sidebar entry.
  - Added `signalsApi.getFastReviewStockOverview(...)` and frontend response types for the stock overview API.
  - Added `PersonalStrategyMatrixService` as the backend registry/read layer for current personal strategies and their signal aliases.
  - Added `GET /api/v1/signals/personal-strategy-matrix`
    - returns the strategy registry
    - returns stock rows with `matched_strategies / matched_strategy_ids`
    - supports optional `code` and `strategy_ids` filtering
  - Added `PersonalStrategiesPage`:
    - date selector
    - optional stock-code filter
    - top strategy filter chips with per-strategy hit counts
    - compact stock list
    - matched strategy chips per stock
    - chart evidence and earnings evidence summaries
    - collapsible grouped strategy explanations
    - reads strategy catalog and matched strategies from the backend API, not from a frontend static list
  - Added `个人策略界面策略清单.md` to document strategy names, current role, and main judgment logic.

## 2026-07-10 (stock-centered fast review query API)

- Scope: `src/services/fast_review_focus_service.py`, `api/v1/endpoints/signals.py`, `api/v1/schemas/signals.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_focus_api.py`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略目录.md`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略基线.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The daily review direction has shifted from `strategy -> stock list` to `stock -> triggered strategy evidence`.
  - The bundle already writes `fast_review_stock_overview.csv/.md`, but before this change it was still only a generated artifact.
  - The missing piece was a stable read interface that can answer one stock's strategy hits without manually opening multiple strategy exports.
- Changes:
  - Added `FastReviewFocusService.get_stock_overview(...)`
    - locates the latest `fast_review_stock_overview.csv` for a snapshot date
    - returns stock-centered rows with `triggered_strategies / signal_keys`
    - supports optional `code` filtering such as `SZ300475`
    - returns `lane_summary` and `signal_summary`
    - intentionally does not run quote/fundamental network enrichment, so the read path is not tied to `Akshare / Tushare` runtime stability
  - Added `GET /api/v1/signals/fast-review-stock-overview`
    - query params:
      - `snapshot_date`
      - optional `code`
    - response model exposes one row per stock, its review lane, strategy hits, chart evidence, earnings evidence, and context summary
  - Added API/service regression coverage for:
    - all stock overview rows
    - filtered single-stock query
    - code normalization from `SZ300475` to `300475`
- Verification:
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile src/services/fast_review_focus_service.py api/v1/endpoints/signals.py api/v1/schemas/signals.py tests/test_fast_review_focus_api.py`

  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_focus_api.py -q`

## 2026-07-10 (earnings observation tool unlocked for manual watch pool)

- Why:

  - The repo already had `earnings_observation_registry/active`, but that tool was still shaped like a schedule-only snapshot task:

- Changes:

## 2026-07-10 (earnings direct-pass quality floor)

- Scope: `scripts/select_earnings_surprise_candidates.py`, `tests/test_earnings_surprise_signal_flow.py`, `docs/local_strategies/core/earnings_surprise.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - After promoting `fast_review_focus_gate` into strategy output, the remaining issue became very clear:
    - `earnings_surprise` could still pass weak-quality names at the strategy layer
    - the problem was not only downstream reading
    - the widest hole was the `direct score reached` branch in `balanced`
  - In practice, this produced names where:
    - growth/text looked strong enough to lift total score above `55`
    - but `earnings_quality_score` stayed weak and `cycle_phase` was still `unavailable`
    - these names then appeared as raw passed candidates even when `快复盘继续看=0`
- Changes:
  - Updated `scripts/select_earnings_surprise_candidates.py`
    - added a `direct quality floor` for `balanced/strict`
    - `direct pass` now requires:
      - `earnings_quality_signal=True`, or
      - `earnings_quality_score >= 50` and `cycle_phase in {recovering, reaccelerating, expanding, neutral}`
    - rows that hit direct score but fail this floor now become:
      - `failure_reason=direct score reached but missing quality floor`
      - `earnings_strategy_gate_status=blocked_direct_quality_floor`
    - exported metrics now also include:
      - `earnings_direct_quality_floor_required`
      - `earnings_direct_quality_floor_ready`
  - Updated `tests/test_earnings_surprise_signal_flow.py`
    - added a regression case covering a real weak-quality direct-pass shape:
      - score >= `55`
      - strong growth/text
      - weak quality score
      - `cycle_phase=unavailable`
      - expected result now blocks under `balanced`
  - Updated `docs/local_strategies/core/earnings_surprise.md`
    - documented the new `direct pass` quality floor
- Verification:
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile scripts/select_earnings_surprise_candidates.py tests/test_earnings_surprise_signal_flow.py`
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_earnings_surprise_signal_flow.py -k "direct_score_blocks_weak_quality_candidate or fast_review_focus_gate or build_selected_dataframe or resume_from_checkpoint" -q`
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python scripts/select_earnings_surprise_candidates.py --snapshot-date 2026-07-10 --strategy-profile balanced --scan-depth low --recent-event-scope latest_report_period --max-workers 1 --skip-db-persist --output-dir data/manual_runs/earnings_fast_review_focus_20260710_stricter --log-level INFO`

## 2026-07-10 (earnings fast-review gate promoted into strategy output)

- Scope: `scripts/select_earnings_surprise_candidates.py`, `tests/test_earnings_surprise_signal_flow.py`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The previous fast-review tightening only lived in the bundle/read layer.
  - That meant `earnings_surprise` itself still exported one flat candidate list where:
    - pure accounting-score names and true fast-review-worthy names were mixed together
    - downstream bundle had to guess again which earnings-only rows were worth keeping on the homepage
  - If this distinction is real, it should exist in the strategy output itself rather than only in the final reader.
- Changes:
  - Updated `scripts/select_earnings_surprise_candidates.py`
    - added native `fast_review_focus_gate_passed` / `fast_review_focus_gate_reason` fields into strategy records
    - refreshes the gate after candidate evaluation, after priority capital-profile enrichment, and after market-expectation enrichment/skip markers
    - `build_selected_dataframe(...)` now sorts fast-review-ready earnings names ahead of score-only names
    - markdown export now shows:
      - total `适合快复盘继续看`
      - per-row `快复盘` yes/no column
    - fixed a regression introduced during this round where:
      - `EarningsSurpriseEvaluation.to_checkpoint_record(...)`
      - `EarningsSurpriseEvaluation.from_checkpoint_record(...)`
      were accidentally nested inside `_refresh_fast_review_focus_gate_metrics(...)`
      and therefore no longer existed as real class methods
  - Updated `tests/test_earnings_surprise_signal_flow.py`
    - added regression coverage that a `fast_review_focus_gate_passed=True` row ranks ahead of a higher raw earnings score row when building the selected dataframe
- Verification:
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile scripts/select_earnings_surprise_candidates.py tests/test_earnings_surprise_signal_flow.py`
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_earnings_surprise_signal_flow.py -k "fast_review_focus_gate or build_selected_dataframe or resume_from_checkpoint" -q`

## 2026-07-10 (stock-centered fast review alignment)

- Scope: `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人每日复盘约定.md`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略基线.md`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略目录.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The latest feedback was no longer just “which single strategy should be tightened again”.
  - The deeper problem was structural:
    - the user naturally reviews stocks, not isolated strategy files
    - a stock may simultaneously carry earnings, chart, board, and imagination-space evidence
    - but the current daily bundle still leaned too much toward `strategy -> stock list`, especially underneath `strategy_focus`
  - In particular, pure `daily_slow_rise / long_base_release` names could still be underrepresented in the stock-level focus layer unless they overlapped with other stronger default signals.
- Changes:
  - Updated `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`
    - expanded the stock-level focus grouping to include:
      - `daily_slow_rise`
      - `long_base_release`
    - pure chart-extension names are now eligible for `strategy_focus` / curated daily review as standalone stocks, instead of only appearing when they overlap with other primary signals
    - added chart-extension-aware tier/score handling so:
      - `daily_slow_rise` rows with usable structure can surface as stock-level watch names
      - `long_base_release` rows that also pass the existing pure-chart read gate can be treated as real stock evidence rather than orphan raw exports
    - added a new stock-centered artifact:
      - `fast_review_stock_overview.csv`
      - `fast_review_stock_overview.md`
    - the new overview keeps one row per stock and summarizes:
      - lane (`双确认 / 图形优先 / 业绩优先 / 趋势补充`)
      - hit strategy count and signal keys
      - chart evidence
      - earnings evidence
      - snapshot and one-line reason
    - added a matching read gate for pure `earnings-only` names on the homepage / stock overview:
      - raw `earnings_surprise` exports are still preserved
      - but stock-level reading now requires more than “accounting score passed”
      - current read gate expects:
        - earnings score high enough
        - revenue / net profit growth not too weak
        - earnings quality score not too low
        - cycle phase not clearly `mixed/mature`
        - and at least one extra confirmation from `capital_profile / relative_strength / institution expectation count`
    - fixed the stock-level bundle field propagation for earnings rows so `revenue_yoy / net_profit_yoy / roe / earnings_quality_cycle_phase` are no longer dropped during CSV load + focus aggregation
  - Updated personal strategy docs
    - recorded that daily reading should default to `summary -> stock overview -> raw signal exports`
    - clarified that future iteration should prefer strengthening the stock aggregation layer instead of continuing to split similar semantics across isolated strategy homepages
- Verification:
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -k "pure_daily_and_long_base or stock_overview or main_writes_strategy_focus_outputs" -q`
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -k "curated_daily_review or filters_weak_pure_long_base_rows_from_chart_first" -q`
- Notes:
  - Offline replay using the already-exported `2026-07-09` signal CSVs:
    - before the earnings-only homepage read gate, stock overview had `29` names (`业绩优先=28`, `图形优先=1`)
    - after the new gate, stock overview narrowed to `13` names (`业绩优先=12`, `图形优先=1`)
  - The remaining issue is now easier to read:
    - architecture is closer to the intended `stock -> evidence`
    - but the underlying earnings strategy still produces many mature / low-imagination names, so future work likely needs real strategy-semantic tightening, not only read-layer polish
  - A full real-world `run_fast_review_bundle.py --safe-mode --snapshot-date 2026-07-09` replay was started, but it was still dominated by the existing `Akshare EM backoff / board ranking` runtime instability and was stopped before end-to-end artifact rewrite completed.
  - That runtime issue is separate from this stock-centered aggregation change and remains a good next target if we continue.

    - kept raw long-base pattern selection logic intact, but improved export ordering so cleaner pure-chart names naturally float above weaker ones

    - homepage `每日精选复盘` now filters out pure long-base names when they fail chart quality or when the latest trade date is stale relative to the replay `snapshot_date`

## 2026-07-09 (Akshare no-data semantics after Tushare empty history)

- Scope: `data_provider/akshare_fetcher.py`, `tests/test_fetcher_logging.py`, `tests/test_kline_fast_manager.py`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - After the previous fast-scan fixes, the main `daily_slow_rise` bottleneck was no longer `Tushare` token/config or the old “skip Akshare after no-data” short-circuit.
  - The remaining noisy failure mode was narrower:
    - `Tushare` returns empty history for a symbol
    - fallback reaches `Akshare`
    - `Akshare EM` is already in backoff from an earlier transient disconnect
    - the remaining free channels only return empty history
    - manager then keeps counting those follow-up misses as `AkshareFetcher` failures and can unnecessarily open the higher-level circuit
- Changes:
  - Updated `data_provider/akshare_fetcher.py`
    - track per-channel outcomes for A-share history fetches
    - when all remaining outcomes are `empty` / `backoff` and there is no fresh hard error in the current attempt, raise a no-data style `DataFetchError` (`未获取到`) instead of the old generic `Akshare 所有渠道获取失败: None`
    - keep real transient transport errors as hard failures so the first genuine EM problem is still visible
  - Added regression coverage:
    - `Tushare no data -> Akshare fallback -> EM backoff` should not keep accumulating into manager-level circuit-open state
    - fast A-share manager tests now align with the current `Tushare first, Akshare fallback` construction
- Verification:
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile data_provider/akshare_fetcher.py tests/test_fetcher_logging.py tests/test_kline_fast_manager.py`
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_fetcher_logging.py -k "tushare_no_data_plus_akshare_em_backoff_does_not_open_manager_circuit" -q`
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_kline_fast_manager.py -q`

## 2026-07-09 (daily review narrowed to one curated document)

- Scope: `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `config/local_strategy_profile.json`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人每日复盘约定.md`, `docs/LOCAL_STRATEGY_CATALOG.md`, `docs/LOCAL_STRATEGY_BASELINE.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The user feedback on the current personal strategy set was consistent:
    - too many strategy names were visible at once
    - the homepage still felt like a bundle of raw strategy exports instead of one readable replay page
    - even when many signals were available, the actual decision support for “图形 + 业绩” was not sharp enough
  - The previous default daily profile still included `trend_leader` in the main include set, which made trend-only rows too visible relative to cleaner chart-driven and earnings-confirmed names.
- Changes:
  - Updated `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`
    - changed the primary summary title from generic fast-review summary to `每日精选复盘`
    - the main document is now organized by reading intent instead of per-strategy artifact order:
      - `今日最值得看`
      - `双确认候选`
      - `图形优先候选`
      - `业绩优先候选`
      - `趋势补充观察`
    - removed top-of-file exposure of multiple `csv/md` artifact paths from the main summary page
    - kept raw `csv/md/txt` artifacts on disk for debugging and later drill-down
    - added lane classification so trend-only rows are displayed as supplementary watch rather than default homepage core
  - Updated `config/local_strategy_profile.json`
    - default `include_signals` is now narrowed to:
      - `earnings`
      - `hundred_day_high`
      - `daily_slow_rise`
      - `long_base_release`
    - `trend_leader` remains supported, but is no longer in the default homepage bundle
  - Updated `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`
    - default include-signal assertions now follow the narrowed daily set
    - summary tests now verify curated reading sections instead of legacy per-strategy spotlight blocks
  - Added [`docs/个人策略文档/个人每日复盘约定.md`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\docs\个人策略文档\个人每日复盘约定.md)
    - records the daily replay goal, default signal set, and future guardrails for AI follow-up

## 2026-07-09 (shared Tushare limiter + no-data fallback preserved for fast scans)

- Scope: `data_provider/tushare_fetcher.py`, `data_provider/base.py`, `src/services/kline_selector_service.py`, `tests/test_tushare_fetcher_followups.py`, `tests/test_fetcher_logging.py`, `tests/test_kline_selector_service.py`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人每日复盘约定.md`, `docs/个人策略文档/个人策略目录.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - After the local `.env` was fixed and `Tushare` truly became available, the next runtime bottlenecks became much clearer:
    - fast scans still let multiple `TushareFetcher` instances count minute quotas independently, so the overall process could still hit the account-side `50/min` limit
    - the fast A-share manager still had a path that could suppress `Tushare no data -> Akshare fallback`, which is exactly the wrong behavior for recent listings and partial provider coverage gaps
  - The user goal for personal strategies is not only “run successfully once”, but “long-term low-concurrency local replay that covers as much filtered universe as practical”.
- Changes:
  - Updated `data_provider/tushare_fetcher.py`
    - moved Tushare minute-budget bookkeeping to a process-wide shared limiter
    - added provider-side rate-limit cooldown arming when the upstream error text clearly indicates frequency throttling
    - kept the conservative configured limit behavior, but stopped allowing each instance to burn the quota independently
  - Updated `src/services/kline_selector_service.py`
    - `build_fast_a_share_manager()` now instantiates the fast-scan `TushareFetcher` with a lower `35/min` headroom
    - fast-scan manager no longer enables the old “redundant no-data fallback skip” path
  - Updated `data_provider/base.py`
    - `TushareFetcher -> AkshareFetcher` no-data path is no longer short-circuited in the fast-scan helper
  - Added regression coverage:
    - shared Tushare budget across fetcher instances
    - provider-side rate-limit cooldown arming
    - fast-scan helper no longer skipping `Akshare` after `Tushare no data`
    - fast A-share manager keeps redundant-fallback suppression disabled and uses the tightened Tushare rate
- Verification:
  - `python -m py_compile data_provider/tushare_fetcher.py data_provider/base.py src/services/kline_selector_service.py tests/test_tushare_fetcher_followups.py tests/test_fetcher_logging.py tests/test_kline_selector_service.py`
  - `python -m pytest tests/test_tushare_fetcher_followups.py -k "shared_across_fetcher_instances or process_wide_cooldown" -q`
  - `python -m pytest tests/test_fetcher_logging.py -k "fast_scan_manager_keeps_akshare_fallback_after_tushare_no_data" -q`
  - `python -m pytest tests/test_kline_selector_service.py -k "build_fast_a_share_manager_keeps_tushare_history_fallback_enabled" -q`
  - real run:
    - `python scripts/select_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py --snapshot-date 2026-07-09 --signal-type daily_slow_rise --profile accelerating --output-dir data/manual_runs/daily_slow_rise_20260709_after_fallback_fix --max-workers 1 --log-level INFO`
      - result: still `0` candidates, but log path now clearly keeps `Tushare no data -> Akshare fallback`
    - `python scripts/select_long_base_release_candidates.py --snapshot-date 2026-07-09 --signal-type long_base_release --profile loose --output-dir data/manual_runs/long_base_release_20260709_shared_limit_fix --max-workers 1 --log-level INFO`
      - result: `selected_count=8`
      - no observed `Tushare daily 50/min` frequency-limit error in this verification run

## 2026-07-09 (fast review weak trend-only demotion + long-base loose default)

- Scope: `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `config/local_strategy_profile.json`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `docs/LOCAL_STRATEGY_CATALOG.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The latest safe-mode fast review replay for `2026-07-09` completed stably, but the front page still surfaced names like `大族激光` and `中国巨石` too high in `strategy_focus`.
  - The user feedback was directionally correct:
    - those rows still satisfied the broader trend selector
    - but their recent price confirmation was already weaker than fresher same-day momentum names
  - At the same time, fast review still defaulted `long_base_release` to `default`, which kept that line too tight to contribute meaningfully on most days.
- Changes:
  - Updated `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`
    - changed fast-review default `DEFAULT_LONG_BASE_RELEASE_PROFILE` from `default` to `loose`
    - added a dedicated trend-only recent-strength adjustment in `strategy_focus` scoring
    - the adjustment now:
      - penalizes weak same-day change
      - penalizes failed `trend_stage2`
      - penalizes negative / weak `bias_ma5`
      - penalizes non-near-high `pullback` rows that only show weak repair
      - rewards strong same-day momentum and healthier trend confirmation for pure trend rows
    - tightened pure `trend_only` tier/score semantics so bundle-level `earnings_signal_active` no longer boosts unrelated trend-only rows just because another stock in the same run hit the earnings signal
    - propagated supporting trend diagnostics into `strategy_focus` rows:
      - `primary_profile`
      - `trend_stage2_passed`
      - `bias_ma5`
      - `near_new_high`
  - Updated `config/local_strategy_profile.json`
    - repo default fast-review profile now uses `long_base_release_profile=loose`
  - Added regression coverage in `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`
    - parse-args defaults now assert `loose`
    - a new focus-ordering test locks that a fresh 10% trend-only row outranks a weak pullback-style trend-only row
- Verification:
  - `python -m py_compile scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`
  - `python -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -k "long_base_release_profile or weak_trend_only" -q`

## 2026-07-09 (local-first strategy cache warming + report-period-aware earnings reuse)

- Scope: `data_provider/base.py`, `scripts/warm_local_strategy_cache.py`, `tests/test_fundamental_context.py`, `docs/LOCAL_STRATEGY_CATALOG.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The local strategy workflow already had usable disk caches for:
    - daily K-line history
    - earnings-only fundamental context
  - But the operational flow still had two gaps:
    - there was no dedicated low-concurrency tool to proactively warm a stable local sample pool before daily replay
    - earnings disk cache reuse still depended on plain TTL, so the same current-quarter payload could be re-fetched repeatedly even when the underlying report period had not changed
  - The user explicitly wanted a local-first mode:
    - cache K lines and earnings data on disk
    - reuse local data by default
    - only pay network cost when freshness or report-period semantics actually require it
- Changes:
  - Updated `data_provider/base.py`
    - added report-period normalization/extraction helpers for earnings fundamental context disk cache
    - extended `get_earnings_fundamental_context(...)` with optional `report_period_hint`
    - when normal disk cache TTL has expired, `ok/partial` earnings context can now still be reused from disk if cached `report_date / report_period` matches the requested report period
    - stale reuse is additionally gated by the latest available earnings announcement anchor; if a newer report / quick report / forecast disclosure exists, stale cache is no longer reused
    - failed-context disk cache does not use this long-lived reuse path and keeps the shorter failure TTL behavior
  - Added `scripts/warm_local_strategy_cache.py`
    - builds a filtered A-share sample pool suitable for local replay
    - defaults to excluding:
      - `ST`
      - `科创板`
      - `北交所`
    - ranks samples with a lightweight liquidity + momentum heuristic
    - warms:
      - daily K-line history cache
      - earnings-only fundamental context cache
    - feeds current-period latest announcement-date hints into the earnings warming path, so same-period stale caches do not mask newly disclosed earnings data
    - when `snapshot-date` equals the current effective trading date, the script now refreshes the current-period earnings event catalog with a same-day max-age policy before warming earnings caches
  - Updated `scripts/select_earnings_surprise_candidates.py`
    - `build_recent_earnings_event_catalog(...)` now supports same-day cache age control, so same-snapshot event catalog reads can be refreshed when local cache has become too old during the trading day
    - persists ranked samples and warm results under:
      - `data/runtime/cache_prewarm/<snapshot-date>/`
  - Added tests in `tests/test_fundamental_context.py`
    - stale earnings disk cache is reused when the requested report period still matches cached content
    - stale earnings disk cache is not reused when the requested report period mismatches
- Verification:
  - `python -m py_compile data_provider/base.py scripts/warm_local_strategy_cache.py`
  - `python -m pytest tests/test_fundamental_context.py -k "stale_disk_cache or reuses_disk_cache" -q`

## 2026-07-08 (fast review low-concurrency safe mode + stable history priority)

- Scope: `src/services/kline_selector_service.py`, `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `data_provider/base.py`, `src/config.py`, `docs/LOCAL_STRATEGY_CATALOG.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The live fast-review run for `2026-07-08` no longer had the old stale-date problem, but it exposed a more urgent operational issue on the Linux single-machine environment:
    - too many Python child processes could accumulate during full-market daily review
    - the machine could become visibly sluggish or close to unusable
  - Root causes found during this round:
    - `DataFetcherManager.get_daily_data(...)` had two manager-level regressions:
      - missing history cache / failed-cache helper methods
      - wrong method dispatch that effectively tried to resolve a stock code like `000001` as a fetcher attribute
    - after those were repaired, the remaining stability problem was runtime-path selection:
      - the specialized full-market fast-scan manager still preferred `Akshare` history first
      - the bundle entry still lacked a first-class “safe low-concurrency mode”, so each run had to rely on manual worker overrides
    - additionally, empty/no-data daily-history responses from upstream could be counted as real source failures, poisoning the manager circuit breaker during broad scans
- Changes:
  - Updated [`data_provider/base.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\data_provider\base.py)
    - restored manager-side history disk cache / failed-cache helper methods needed by broad `get_daily_data(...)` reuse
    - fixed manager dispatch so daily-data calls now go through the correct fetcher history method instead of attempting attribute lookup by stock code
    - tightened daily-source failure accounting so obvious “no data / empty result” responses do not incorrectly trip source-health degradation
  - Updated [`src/config.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\src\config.py)
    - `tushare_token` now accepts environment aliases:
      - `TUSHARE_TOKEN`
      - `TUSHARE_PRO_TOKEN`
      - `TUSHARE_API_TOKEN`
    - realtime-source priority bootstrap now uses the same alias resolution, so Linux and Windows env naming mismatches no longer silently disable `tushare`
  - Updated [`src/services/kline_selector_service.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\src\services\kline_selector_service.py)
    - `build_fast_a_share_manager()` now prefers `Tushare` first when token is available
    - keeps `Akshare` attached as lightweight fallback only
    - retains reduced Akshare history-path aggressiveness (`tencent/em`, retry=1) for the fast full-market scan path
  - Updated [`scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\scripts\run_fast_review_bundle.py)
    - added `--safe-mode`
    - when enabled, it clamps all relevant bundle and per-signal worker counts to `1`, including:
      - bundle `external_parallelism`
      - generic `max_workers`
      - `earnings`, `hundred_day_high`, `monthly_slow_rise`, `daily_slow_rise`, `long_base_release`, `trend_leader`, and `continuous_up` worker settings
- Verification:
  - `./.venv-linux/bin/python -m py_compile src/config.py data_provider/base.py data_provider/akshare_fetcher.py src/services/kline_selector_service.py scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`
  - token alias check:
    - `TUSHARE_PRO_TOKEN=... ./.venv-linux/bin/python - <<'PY' ...`
    - confirmed config can resolve token and realtime priority includes `tushare`
  - manager smoke:
    - `TUSHARE_PRO_TOKEN=... ./.venv-linux/bin/python - <<'PY' ... DataFetcherManager().get_daily_data('000001', end_date='2026-05-19', days=30, force_refresh=True) ...`
    - returned non-empty rows from `TushareFetcher`

## 2026-05-19 (fast review trend leader diagnostics persistence)

- Scope: `scripts/select_trend_leader_candidates.py`, `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `tests/test_trend_leader_signal_flow.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, `docs/LOCAL_STRATEGY_CATALOG.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The latest `2026-05-19` replay regression was no longer a candidate-count problem.
  - Measured evidence from replay artifacts showed:
    - `daily_slow_rise` kept the same `universe=1438 / processed=1314`, but `history_fetch_avg` rose from `0.1675s` to `0.2028s`
    - `hundred_day_high` kept the same `universe=678 / processed=629`, but `history_fetch_avg` rose from `0.0872s` to `0.1074s`
    - selected `trend_leader` rows also showed worse `history_fetch` and `fundamental_fetch`, while `_fundamental_cache_source=disk` dropped from `5` hits to `0`
  - The missing piece was observability: fast review persisted full checkpoint/summary for `hundred_day_high` and `daily_slow_rise`, but `trend_leader` still exported only candidate CSV/TXT, which made future regressions harder to attribute.
- Changes:
  - Updated [`scripts/select_trend_leader_candidates.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\scripts\select_trend_leader_candidates.py)
    - `export_results(...)` now writes `trend_leader_unified_run_summary.json`
    - when a checkpoint path is supplied, the exported run now also persists `trend_leader_unified_checkpoint.json`
    - the summary stores `selected_count`, `watch_selected_count`, and raw `run_stats`, so later analysis can directly inspect `processed_count`, `phase_timing_sec`, cache-hit counts, and elapsed splits
  - Updated [`scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\scripts\run_fast_review_bundle.py)
    - `build_trend_leader_command(...)` now passes an explicit checkpoint path under the day-level fast-review signal directory
    - this makes trend-leader replays leave behind the same kind of diagnostic artifacts already available for the other K-line strategies
- Verification:
  - `python -m py_compile scripts/select_trend_leader_candidates.py scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py tests/test_trend_leader_signal_flow.py tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`
  - `E:\Apps\daily_stock_analysis\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_trend_leader_signal_flow.py -k export_results_writes_watchlist_sidecar_files -q`
  - `E:\Apps\daily_stock_analysis\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -k test_build_commands_forward_skip_db_persist -q`

## 2026-05-19 (fast review authority structured settle before broad search)

- Scope: `src/services/signal_cause_analysis_service.py`, `tests/test_signal_cause_analysis_service.py`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - After the shared cause-service reuse change, the next explanation-stage hotspot was the authority fallback path itself.
  - Root cause:
    - `_collect_authority_intel(...)` fetched structured `announcements / earnings / market_analysis`
    - but as long as the three dimensions were not all present, it still escalated into `search_comprehensive_intel(...)`
    - this meant many fast-review rows with already sufficient structured earnings or announcement evidence still paid for a broad multi-dimension search
- Changes:
  - Updated [`src/services/signal_cause_analysis_service.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\src\services\signal_cause_analysis_service.py)
    - `_collect_structured_evidence(...)` now passes `fundamental_context` and `metrics_payload` into `_collect_authority_intel(...)`
    - `_collect_authority_intel(...)` now short-circuits before broad search when structured evidence is already enough to settle authority at:
      - strong announcement
      - authoritative current-season earnings confirmation
      - sufficiently strong structured research summary
    - the broad fallback remains in place for rows that still need missing authority evidence
  - Updated [`tests/test_signal_cause_analysis_service.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\tests\test_signal_cause_analysis_service.py)
    - added `test_collect_authority_intel_skips_fallback_search_when_structured_earnings_already_confirm`
    - added `test_collect_authority_intel_skips_fallback_search_when_strong_announcement_already_exists`
- Verification:
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m py_compile src/services/signal_cause_analysis_service.py tests/test_signal_cause_analysis_service.py`
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_signal_cause_analysis_service.py tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -q`
  - real replay:
    - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\run_fast_review_bundle.py --strategy-profile-file config\local_strategy_profile.json --snapshot-date 2026-05-19 --output-dir data/manual_runs/fast_review_20260519_today_review_after_authority_settle_20260519 --log-level INFO`

    - summary signal elapsed: `285.5s`
    - comparison vs prior shared-cause-service replay:
      - `315.7s -> 285.5s` signal elapsed
      - `trend_leader 154.20s -> 129.86s`
      - `daily_slow_rise 84.69s -> 78.51s`
      - `hundred_day_high 76.79s -> 77.17s` (roughly flat)

## 2026-05-19 (fast review shared cause-service cache reuse)

- Scope: `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - After the earlier quote-tail cleanup, the next likely explanation-stage bottleneck was not quote retry any more but repeated authority cache warmup.
  - Root cause found in bundle assembly:
    - `_build_strategy_focus_rows(...)` called `_resolve_focus_rise_reason_fields(...)`
    - that helper instantiated a brand-new `SignalCauseAnalysisService(enable_news_search=False)` per row
    - this discarded in-instance caches such as:
      - structured announcement day cache
      - structured research cache
      - earnings catalog cache
  - For same-day fast review, this meant multiple rows in one bundle were paying repeated authority bootstrapping cost instead of sharing it.
- Changes:
  - Updated [`scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\scripts\run_fast_review_bundle.py)
    - `_build_strategy_focus_rows(...)` now creates one shared `SignalCauseAnalysisService(enable_news_search=False)` for the whole focus-row build
    - `_resolve_focus_rise_reason_fields(...)` now accepts an optional injected `cause_service`
    - per-row fallback behavior is preserved: if no service is injected, the helper still instantiates one for isolated use
  - Updated [`tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\tests\test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py)
    - added `test_build_strategy_focus_rows_reuses_one_cause_service_instance_per_bundle`
    - kept existing alias refresh / missing-reason / fail-open regressions green to confirm behavior did not change
- Verification:
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -k "reuses_one_cause_service_instance_per_bundle or refreshes_existing_reason_fields_for_alias_names or enriches_missing_reason_fields or fails_open_when_reason_enrichment_errors" -q`
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m py_compile scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -q`

## 2026-05-19 (fast review focus quote-tail dedupe)

- Scope: `src/services/fast_review_focus_service.py`, `tests/test_fast_review_focus_api.py`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The latest `2026-05-19` real fast-review run still had a visible tail in `FastReviewFocusService` quote enrichment.
  - Root cause split into two redundant retry patterns:
    - when `Akshare EM` cold-start warmup immediately returned empty, `_load_batch_quote_payloads(...)` still kept walking every remaining A-share code through the same empty cache path
    - when Tencent batch prefetch had already tried a code and still missed, `_resolve_quote_payload(...)` retried the same lightweight Tencent call again per row before falling through
- Changes:
  - Updated [`src/services/fast_review_focus_service.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\src\services\fast_review_focus_service.py)
    - `_load_batch_quote_payloads(...)` now stops repeated `EM` reads after a cold-start warmup miss, and jumps directly to Tencent batch prefetch for the unresolved remainder
    - batch quote loading now tracks which codes already went through Tencent prefetch
    - `_resolve_quote_payload(...)` now skips the redundant per-row lightweight Tencent retry for codes that already missed in the batch stage
    - `_is_em_quote_cache_warm()` now treats empty cached payloads as not truly warm/usable for quote reuse
  - Updated [`tests/test_fast_review_focus_api.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\tests\test_fast_review_focus_api.py)
    - added `test_focus_service_skips_repeated_em_reads_after_cold_warmup_miss`
    - added `test_focus_service_does_not_repeat_lightweight_tencent_after_batch_miss`
- Verification:
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_focus_api.py -k "skips_repeated_em_reads_after_cold_warmup_miss or does_not_repeat_lightweight_tencent_after_batch_miss" -q`

## 2026-05-19 (daily slow-rise fetch-buffer fix)

- Scope: `scripts/select_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py`, `tests/test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py`, `docs/LOCAL_STRATEGY_CATALOG.md`, and `docs/CHANGELOG.md`.
- Why:
  - The first real `daily_slow_rise` probe was not failing on chart quality first; it was collapsing on history-window edge cases.
  - The concrete evidence was:
    - `32/32` failed in the first `limit=200` probe
    - `30` were `insufficient daily history`
    - a direct re-check showed history length distribution `{49: 1, 58: 1, 59: 30}`
  - That meant the strategy was requesting an exact 60-bar window, while the fast manager often returned one bar short in real cached/full-market conditions.
- Changes:
  - Updated [`scripts/select_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\scripts\select_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py)
    - changed `DailySlowRiseCriteria.history_days_required` from the exact 60-bar rule window to a buffered fetch window of `62`
    - kept the actual pattern semantics unchanged: the rule still judges the latest 30-bar base + 30-bar advance shape
  - Updated [`tests/test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\tests\test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py)
    - added a regression test that locks the buffered default at `62`
    - extended the synthetic chart fixtures with two extra prefix bars so the strategy tests still exercise real shape rules instead of failing early on history length
- Verification:
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py -q`
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m py_compile scripts/select_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py tests/test_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py`
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_daily_slow_rise_fast_review_integration.py tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -k daily_slow_rise -q`
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts/select_daily_slow_rise_candidates.py --snapshot-date 2026-05-19 --skip-db-persist --limit 200 --max-workers 2 --output-dir data/manual_runs/daily_slow_rise_20260519_probe_buffer_fix_200 --checkpoint-path data/manual_runs/daily_slow_rise_20260519_probe_buffer_fix_200/daily_slow_rise_checkpoint.json --log-level INFO`
- Result:
  - The same real probe no longer died on history length.
  - Failure distribution changed from mostly `insufficient daily history` to real chart-quality reasons:
    - `single-day gain too large`: `14`
    - `average daily amount too low`: `11`
    - `drawdown too deep`: `6`
    - `insufficient history: need about 62 trading days, got 52`: `1`
  - A direct re-check after the fix showed history length distribution `{52: 1, 61: 1, 62: 30}`.
- Notes:
  - This round fixed a fetch-window bug, not the final shape thresholds.
  - The next threshold-tuning round should focus on whether `single-day gain too large` and `average daily amount too low` are too strict for the user’s target “healthy 30-45 degree rise” names.

## 2026-05-15 (fast-review spot cache fallback no longer blocks on listing metadata)

- Scope: continue the fast-review runtime investigation after the external-heartbeat watchdog fix, and remove the next confirmed startup bottleneck shared by `earnings_surprise`, `hundred_day_high`, and `trend_leader_unified`.
- Why:
  - After the bundle-layer watchdog fix, small-scope probes still showed all three selectors spending their early runtime inside `src/services/kline_selector_service.py::get_spot_enriched_a_share_universe()`.
  - Direct timing and staged probes confirmed the shared failure chain:
    - fresh spot reference cache was empty
    - stale disk spot reference cache existed and loaded quickly
    - live `spot` fetch failed after about `5-6s`
    - old fallback logic then still tried to enrich that stale cache with `listing metadata`
    - `tushare listing meta` failed quickly due rate limit, but `baostock listing meta` could hang for `20s+`
  - This meant the daily fast-review startup path kept paying a long tail even after it had already decided to accept stale/disk `spot` cache as the quote fallback.
- Root cause:
  - `get_spot_enriched_a_share_universe()` treated stale/disk `spot` cache as a usable quote fallback, but still forced a best-effort `listing metadata` merge on top of that cache.
  - In the current Windows/network environment, the `listing metadata` provider chain was the real long tail:
    - `tushare` -> fast rate-limit failure
    - `baostock` -> slow/hanging fallback
- Changes:
  - Updated [`src/services/kline_selector_service.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\src\services\kline_selector_service.py)
    - when `prefer_stale_spot_universe_reference_cache` is used, stale/disk `spot` cache is now accepted directly as the fast path
    - when live `spot` fetch fails and the service falls back to disk cached `spot` snapshot, it no longer blocks on `listing metadata` merge before returning that fallback snapshot
    - generic-provider fallback still keeps the old `listing metadata` merge behavior
  - Updated [`tests/test_kline_selector_service.py`](d:\bb\daily_stock_analysis\tests\test_kline_selector_service.py)
    - added regression coverage that incomplete stale preferred cache should no longer trigger listing-metadata fetch
    - added regression coverage that incomplete stale disk-cache failure fallback should no longer trigger listing-metadata fetch
- Verification:
  - Red:
    - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_kline_selector_service.py -k "incomplete_stale_disk_cache or incomplete_stale_disk_reference_cache"`
    - result before fix: `2 failed`
    - failure showed `_fetch_listing_dates_dataframe()` was still being called in both stale-cache paths
  - Green:
    - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_kline_selector_service.py -k "incomplete_stale_disk_cache or incomplete_stale_disk_reference_cache or can_prefer_stale_disk_reference_cache or uses_stale_disk_cache_for_failure_fallback"`
    - result: `4 passed`
  - Runtime probes:
    - preferred stale cache path:
      - `prefer_cache_get_spot_enriched ok 0.21s len 1`
    - default path with live spot failure:
      - `default_get_spot_enriched ok 6.766s len 1`
      - this confirms the long `listing metadata` hang is gone and runtime is now dominated by the single live-spot failure attempt
    - end-to-end `limit=1` probes after fix:
      - `trend_leader`: universe prepare now completes and exits normally in about `1-2s` after startup logs
      - `earnings_surprise`: completes `limit=1` run in about `8s`
      - `hundred_day_high`: completes `limit=1` run in about `6-7s`
- Notes:
  - This round deliberately does not change the selector thresholds or daily strategy profile.
  - Residual startup cost still exists on selectors that first attempt a live `spot` fetch before falling back to disk cache; the remaining cost is now the `live spot` failure itself rather than `listing metadata` tail blocking.

## 2026-05-15 (fast-review external heartbeat watchdog)

- Scope: fix the false `idle timeout after 1800s` failures observed in `Fast Review` daily runs when external signal scripts stayed alive but went quiet for a long time.
- Why:
  - Real replay evidence on `2026-05-14` showed all three external signals:
    - `earnings_surprise`
    - `hundred_day_high`
    - `trend_leader_unified`
    were started successfully, printed early startup logs, then entered long quiet phases and got killed by the bundle's stdout-idle watchdog.
  - The issue was not script import deadlock:
    - `--help` for all three scripts returned quickly
    - direct module imports also completed quickly
  - Short background probes confirmed the pattern:
    - `earnings`: reached `recent-event prefilter enabled` and `spot-enriched universe fallback to disk cached spot snapshot`, then stayed alive without further output for at least 60s
    - `hundred_day_high`: printed startup plus `spot-enriched universe fallback...`, then stayed alive without further output for at least 60s
    - `trend_leader`: printed startup/config logs, then stayed alive without further output for at least 60s
- Root cause:
  - `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py::_run_command(...)` treated “no child stdout for 1800 seconds” as a hard failure even after the child had already proven liveness by emitting startup logs.
  - For these selectors, legitimate long silent phases exist after startup, so the watchdog semantics were too aggressive.
- Changes:
  - Updated `scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py`
    - added `DEFAULT_EXTERNAL_COMMAND_HEARTBEAT_SEC` / `EXTERNAL_COMMAND_HEARTBEAT_SEC`
    - added CLI/profile arg `--external-command-heartbeat-sec` (default `300`)
    - once an external child has emitted at least one output line, the bundle now:
      - emits parent-side `external heartbeat` lines every `heartbeat_sec`
      - no longer treats later silence as `idle timeout`
      - still enforces `total timeout`
    - `idle timeout` is now kept only for the stricter case where the child never emitted any output at all
  - Updated `tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`
    - preserved the regression that a completely silent child still raises `idle timeout`
    - added coverage that a child which prints one startup line and then sleeps is no longer killed by idle timeout and now emits heartbeat text
    - added parse/default assertions for `external_command_heartbeat_sec`
- Verification:
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -k "no_output_timeout or post_start_silence_with_heartbeat" -q`
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py -k "fast_review_defaults_when_profile_missing or reads_fast_review_defaults_from_profile or post_start_silence_with_heartbeat or no_output_timeout" -q`
  - `.\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m py_compile scripts/run_fast_review_bundle.py tests/test_fast_review_daily_bundle.py`
- Notes:
  - This round fixes the watchdog semantics in the bundle layer; it does not yet add child-side heartbeat logs inside each selector's deep scan loop.
  - Residual risk remains that a truly hung child which already printed startup logs will now wait until `total timeout` instead of `idle timeout`.

## 2026-05-11 (fast-review explicit snapshot-date rollback fix)

- Scope:
  - fix the daily fast-review date split where `trend_leader` and `earnings` silently rolled an explicit trading date back to the previous session
  - keep holiday fallback behavior, but stop rolling back an explicitly requested trading day like `2026-05-11`
- Root cause:
  - `scripts/select_trend_leader_candidates.py` and `scripts/select_earnings_surprise_candidates.py` both parsed `--snapshot-date` through `get_effective_trading_date("cn", current_time=datetime.combine(parsed, datetime.min.time()))`
  - that means an explicit trading day was reinterpreted as `00:00` intraday, so the trading-calendar helper treated it as “market not closed yet” and returned the previous completed session
  - `scripts/select_hundred_day_high_candidates.py` does not do this rollback, so the bundle could end up with:
    - `trend_leader = 2026-05-08`
    - `earnings = 2026-05-08`
    - `hundred_day_high = 2026-05-11`
- Changes:
  - Updated `scripts/select_trend_leader_candidates.py`
    - explicit `datetime` input is now forwarded as-is into `get_effective_trading_date(...)`
    - explicit `date` / ISO string input is now resolved with `23:59:59.999999` instead of `00:00:00`
  - Updated `scripts/select_earnings_surprise_candidates.py`
    - same fix as above for explicit `datetime`, `date`, and ISO-string inputs
  - Updated `tests/test_snapshot_date_resolution.py`
    - added regression coverage for explicit trading-day strings on both `trend_leader` and `earnings`
    - the tests assert that the helper receives end-of-day timestamps instead of midnight timestamps
- Verification:
  - Red:
    - `E:\Apps\daily_stock_analysis\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests\test_snapshot_date_resolution.py -q`
    - result before fix: `2 failed, 4 passed`
    - failure showed `current_time == datetime(2026, 5, 11, 0, 0)` instead of `datetime(2026, 5, 11, 23, 59, 59, 999999)`
  - Green:
    - `E:\Apps\daily_stock_analysis\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m pytest tests\test_snapshot_date_resolution.py -q`
      - result: `6 passed`
    - `python -m py_compile scripts\select_trend_leader_candidates.py scripts\select_earnings_surprise_candidates.py tests\test_snapshot_date_resolution.py`
    - real replay:
      - `E:\Apps\daily_stock_analysis\.venv\Scripts\python.exe scripts\run_fast_review_bundle.py --strategy-profile-file config\local_strategy_profile.json --snapshot-date 2026-05-11 --output-dir data\manual_runs\fast_review_20260511_date_fix_verify_20260511_233939 --log-level INFO`
      - replay logs now show:
        - `earnings_surprise_selector: ... snapshot_date=2026-05-11`
        - `trend_leader_selector: ... snapshot_date=2026-05-11`
        - `hundred_day_high_selector: ... snapshot_date=2026-05-11`
      - replay artifact summary now shows:
        - `earnings = 2`
        - `hundred_day_high = 256 (export 30)`
        - `trend_leader = 9`
- Notes:
  - this fix only changes explicit-date resolution for completed daily-review runs
  - no change was made to the holiday/non-trading fallback regression already covered in `tests/test_snapshot_date_resolution.py`

## 2026-05-09 (authority partial-dimension search merge)

- Scope:
  - fix the authority-intel short-circuit so `Fast Review Focus` can keep searching missing authority dimensions even after one structured source already hit
- Why:
  - after the previous authority-summary refinements, the remaining real-world gap was no longer classification wording, but source coverage
  - root cause:
    - `_collect_authority_intel(...)` returned immediately once any structured dimension existed
    - in practice, many rows already had structured `earnings`
    - so `announcements` / `market_analysis` never reached `search_comprehensive_intel(...)`
  - that meant a row could already be `财报确认`, but still lose the more specific “why it is rising” explanation from authority announcement/research text
- Changes:
  - Updated `src/services/signal_cause_analysis_service.py`
    - `_collect_authority_intel(...)` now only short-circuits when:
      - authority search is disabled, or
      - all three dimensions already have structured evidence
    - partial structured hits now continue into the comprehensive search layer and merge only the missing dimensions
  - Updated `tests/test_signal_cause_analysis_service.py`
    - added `test_collect_authority_intel_still_searches_missing_dimensions_when_earnings_exist`
    - regression locks the concrete bug: structured earnings exist, structured research is empty, and search-backed `market_analysis` must still merge in

      - result: `111 passed`
    - `E:\Apps\daily_stock_analysis\.venv\Scripts\python.exe -m py_compile src/services/signal_cause_analysis_service.py tests/test_signal_cause_analysis_service.py`

        - however `research_evidence_summary` stayed `0/51`

## 2026-05-09 (authority catalyst summary refinement)

- Scope:
